ERP SYSTEM ACTIVITY LOG
Tracks all user activities including: Items, Sales, Purchases, Parties, Payments, Stock, Expenses
Format: TIMESTAMP | ACTIVITY_TYPE | ACTION | CompanyInfo UserInfo EntityInfo Details
